                "SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'table' AND name = 'board_outcomes'"
            ).fetchone()
            legacy_entries = (
                int(conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM board_outcomes").fetchone()[0])
                if legacy_table
                else 0
            )
        finally:
            conn.close()
        result = {
            "model_version": MODEL_VERSION,
            "tracked_rows_per_board": self.visible_limit,
            "total_entries": total,
            "collecting": collecting,
            # Ungraded because tracking stopped before their 15-minute mark.
            "interrupted": interrupted,
            "legacy_entries_preserved": legacy_entries,
            "observation_policy": {
                "reentry_cooldown_minutes": int(self.cooldown_seconds / 60),
                "control": "nearest same-direction non-board mover",
                "estimated_round_trip_cost_pct": self.round_trip_cost_pct,
                "minimum_independent_samples": self.minimum_samples,
                "minimum_collection_days": self.minimum_span_days,
            },
            "boards": boards,
        }
        with self._summary_lock:
            self._summary_cache = (time.monotonic(), dict(result))
        return result


store = BoardOutcomeStore()


__all__ = ["BoardOutcomeStore", "BOARD_NAMES", "store"]
===== END SOURCE 09 =====

===== BEGIN SOURCE 10/16: backend/live_ranking.py =====
"""Full-universe live strength ranking for the dashboard.

The score is deliberately descriptive rather than predictive: it ranks the
current tape using only data that is available now.  It never fills missing
market, volume, or alert inputs with fabricated observations.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from math import isfinite
from typing import Any


LIVE_RANKING_MODEL_VERSION = "live-strength-v1"
LIVE_RANKING_SNAPSHOT_VERSION = "live-strength-v1-snapshot-v3"

# Retention-only fields. They are collected solely so a historical outcome can
# be explained later, and must never reach the public ranking payload — the
# frontend contract predates them. build_live_rankings() therefore omits them
# unless a caller opts in, and public_ranking_rows() strips them if it did.
INTERNAL_RANKING_FIELDS = ("raw_inputs", "scoring_detail")

_STABLECOINS = {
    "USDC",
    "USDT",
    "DAI",
    "PYUSD",
    "GUSD",
    "USDS",
    "EURC",
}

_BULLISH_ALERTS = {
    "moonshot",
    "breakout",
    "building",
    "heating",
    "fomo",
    "bullish_reversal",
    "squeeze",
    "trend_break_up",
    "volume_surge",
}

_BEARISH_ALERTS = {
    "crater",
    "dump",
    "critical",
    "cooling",
    "rapid_drop",
    "reversal_risk",
    "failure",
    "trend_break_down",
}


def _finite(value: Any) -> float | None:
    try:
        number = float(value)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None
    return number if isfinite(number) else None


def _symbol(value: Any) -> str:
    raw = str(value or "").strip().upper()
    for suffix in ("-USD", "-USDT", "-USDC", "-PERP"):
        if raw.endswith(suffix):
            return raw[: -len(suffix)]
    return raw


def _clamp(value: float, low: float = 0.0, high: float = 100.0) -> float:
    return max(low, min(high, value))


def _signed_score(value: float | None, full_scale: float) -> float:
    if value is None:
        return 50.0
    return _clamp(50.0 + (50.0 * value / full_scale))


def _label(score: int) -> str:
    if score >= 80:
        return "Leading"
    if score >= 65:
        return "Strong"
    if score >= 55:
        return "Building"
    if score >= 45:
        return "Mixed"
    if score >= 35:
        return "Weak"
    return "Fading"


def public_ranking_rows(rows: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Return copies of ``rows`` with retention-only fields removed.

    Used on the publish path so enabling internal feature collection for
    retention never changes the shape of the payload the frontend receives.
    The originals are left intact for the snapshot writer.
    """
    return [
        {key: value for key, value in row.items() if key not in INTERNAL_RANKING_FIELDS}
        for row in rows
    ]


def build_feature_snapshot(
    ranking_row: dict[str, Any],
    captured_ts: int,
) -> tuple[str, str] | tuple[None, None]:
    """Return (schema_version, json_text) capturing a full point-in-time feature state.

    ``captured_ts`` must be the instant the ranking calculation that produced
    ``ranking_row`` was started — never a later observer timestamp, or the
    stored record would misdate the state it claims to describe.

    Serialised with sort_keys=True so the same input always produces the same bytes.
    Only call with a row that was returned by build_live_rankings() and has already
    had live_rank and universe_size attached (i.e. after the post-sort loop).

    Returns (None, None) when the row was built without internal feature
    collection. A snapshot missing raw_inputs/scoring_detail cannot explain an
    outcome, so it is refused outright rather than stored half-empty.
    """
    if any(field not in ranking_row for field in INTERNAL_RANKING_FIELDS):
        return None, None
    snapshot = {
        "schema_version": LIVE_RANKING_SNAPSHOT_VERSION,
        "captured_ts": captured_ts,
        "model_version": ranking_row.get("model_version"),
        "live_score": ranking_row.get("live_score"),
        "live_label": ranking_row.get("live_label"),
        "live_rank": ranking_row.get("live_rank"),
        "universe_size": ranking_row.get("universe_size"),
        "data_quality": ranking_row.get("data_quality"),
        "observed_inputs": ranking_row.get("observed_inputs"),
        "expected_inputs": ranking_row.get("expected_inputs"),
        "live_risks": ranking_row.get("live_risks"),
        "components": ranking_row.get("live_components"),
        "raw_inputs": ranking_row.get("raw_inputs"),
        "scoring_detail": ranking_row.get("scoring_detail"),
    }
    return LIVE_RANKING_SNAPSHOT_VERSION, json.dumps(snapshot, sort_keys=True)


def _alert_direction(alert: dict[str, Any]) -> int:
    kind = (
        str(
            alert.get("type_key")
            or alert.get("type")
            or alert.get("class_key")
            or alert.get("category")
            or ""
        )
        .strip()
        .lower()
        .replace(" ", "_")
    )
    if kind in _BULLISH_ALERTS:
        return 1
    if kind in _BEARISH_ALERTS:
        return -1

    evidence = alert.get("evidence") if isinstance(alert.get("evidence"), dict) else {}
    for key in ("pct_1m", "pct_3m", "pct_1h", "pct"):
        value = _finite(evidence.get(key))
        if value is not None and value != 0:
            return 1 if value > 0 else -1
    return 0


def build_live_rankings(
    *,
    price_snapshot: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] | None,
    volume_snapshot: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
    context_snapshot: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
    alerts: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
    include_internal_features: bool = False,
    volatility_snapshot: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
    liquidity_snapshot: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Return strongest-to-weakest live setup rows for the tracked universe.

    ``include_internal_features`` adds the retention-only ``raw_inputs`` and
    ``scoring_detail`` keys.  It changes nothing that is scored: both are built
    from values the scorer has already computed.  It defaults to False so the
    published payload keeps its original shape.
    """

    prices = price_snapshot if isinstance(price_snapshot, dict) else {}
    volumes = volume_snapshot if isinstance(volume_snapshot, dict) else {}
    contexts = context_snapshot if isinstance(context_snapshot, dict) else {}
    alert_rows = alerts if isinstance(alerts, list) else []
    volatilities = volatility_snapshot if isinstance(volatility_snapshot, dict) else {}
    liquidities = liquidity_snapshot if isinstance(liquidity_snapshot, dict) else {}

    alert_counts: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {}
    for alert in alert_rows:
        if not isinstance(alert, dict):
            continue
        sym = _symbol(alert.get("symbol") or alert.get("product_id"))
        if not sym:
            continue
        direction = _alert_direction(alert)
        counts = alert_counts.setdefault(sym, {"bullish": 0, "bearish": 0})
        if direction > 0:
            counts["bullish"] += 1
        elif direction < 0:
            counts["bearish"] += 1

    rankings: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for raw_symbol, raw_row in prices.items():
        sym = _symbol(raw_symbol)
        if not sym or sym in _STABLECOINS or not isinstance(raw_row, dict):
            continue

        volume_row = volumes.get(sym) if isinstance(volumes.get(sym), dict) else {}
        context_row = contexts.get(sym) if isinstance(contexts.get(sym), dict) else {}
        one_min = _finite(raw_row.get("pct_1m"))
        three_min = _finite(raw_row.get("pct_3m"))
        one_hour = _finite(raw_row.get("pct_1h"))
        volume_pct = _finite(
            volume_row.get("volume_change_1h_pct")
            if volume_row
            else raw_row.get("volume_change_1h_pct")
        )
        streak = _finite(raw_row.get("trend_streak") or raw_row.get("streak"))

        one_min_score = _signed_score(one_min, 1.25)
        three_min_score = _signed_score(three_min, 3.0)
        one_hour_score = _signed_score(one_hour, 6.0)
        volume_score = _signed_score(volume_pct, 200.0)

        directions = [
            value for value in (one_min, three_min) if value is not None and value != 0
        ]
        if len(directions) >= 2 and all(value > 0 for value in directions):
            confirmation_score = 80.0
        elif len(directions) >= 2 and all(value < 0 for value in directions):
            confirmation_score = 20.0
        elif len(directions) >= 2:
            confirmation_score = 35.0
        elif directions:
            confirmation_score = 60.0 if directions[0] > 0 else 40.0
        else:
            confirmation_score = 50.0

        counts = alert_counts.get(sym, {"bullish": 0, "bearish": 0})
        confirmation_score = _clamp(
            confirmation_score
            + min(18, counts["bullish"] * 6)
            - min(18, counts["bearish"] * 6)
        )
        spot_pressure = str(context_row.get("spot_pressure") or "neutral").lower()
        market_relation = str(context_row.get("market_relation") or "neutral").lower()
        if spot_pressure == "buying":
            confirmation_score += 10.0
        elif spot_pressure == "selling":
            confirmation_score -= 10.0
        if market_relation == "breaking_away":
            confirmation_score += 7.0 if (three_min or one_min or 0) > 0 else -7.0
        confirmation_score = _clamp(confirmation_score)

        dominant_move = three_min if three_min not in (None, 0) else one_min
        dominant_sign = (
            1 if (dominant_move or 0) > 0 else -1 if (dominant_move or 0) < 0 else 0
        )
        persistence_score = 50.0
        if streak is not None and dominant_sign:
            persistence_score = _clamp(
                50.0 + dominant_sign * min(5.0, abs(streak)) * 9.0
            )

        weighted = (
            one_min_score * 0.20
            + three_min_score * 0.25
            + one_hour_score * 0.15
            + volume_score * 0.15
            + confirmation_score * 0.15
            + persistence_score * 0.10
        )

        chase_penalty = 0.0
        if one_min is not None and one_min > 1.6:
            chase_penalty += min(7.0, (one_min - 1.6) * 2.5)
        if three_min is not None and three_min > 4.0:
            chase_penalty += min(7.0, (three_min - 4.0) * 1.5)
        liquidity = str(context_row.get("liquidity") or "normal").lower()
        if liquidity == "thin":
            chase_penalty += 5.0
        elif liquidity == "wide":
            chase_penalty += 2.0

        raw_score = _clamp(weighted - chase_penalty, 1.0, 99.0)
        observed = sum(
            value is not None
            for value in (one_min, three_min, one_hour, volume_pct, streak)
        )
        if counts["bullish"] or counts["bearish"]:
            observed += 1
        if (
            spot_pressure != "neutral"
            or market_relation != "neutral"
            or liquidity != "normal"
        ):
            observed += 1
        expected_inputs = 6
        observed_inputs = min(expected_inputs, observed)
        data_quality = min(100, int(round((observed_inputs / expected_inputs) * 100.0)))
        # Sparse evidence must not beat a genuinely aligned setup simply because
        # one available metric is extreme. Pull incomplete rows toward neutral;
        # keep a small floor so a real early move can still surface as Building.
        reliability = max(0.35, data_quality / 100.0)
        score = int(round(_clamp(50.0 + ((raw_score - 50.0) * reliability), 1.0, 99.0)))

        reasons: list[str] = []
        if three_min is not None:
            reasons.append(f"3m {three_min:+.2f}%")
        if one_min is not None:
            reasons.append(f"1m {one_min:+.2f}%")
        if volume_pct is not None:
            reasons.append(f"1h volume {volume_pct:+.0f}%")
        if spot_pressure == "buying":
            reasons.append("spot buying")
        elif spot_pressure == "selling":
            reasons.append("spot selling")
        if market_relation == "breaking_away":
            reasons.append("breaking away from market")
        if counts["bullish"]:
            reasons.append(
                f"{counts['bullish']} bullish alert{'s' if counts['bullish'] != 1 else ''}"
            )
        if counts["bearish"]:
            reasons.append(
                f"{counts['bearish']} bearish alert{'s' if counts['bearish'] != 1 else ''}"
            )
        if streak is not None and streak > 1:
            reasons.append(f"{int(streak)}x tape hold")

        risks: list[str] = []
        if chase_penalty > 0:
            risks.append("extended move")
        if one_min is not None and three_min is not None and one_min * three_min < 0:
            risks.append("timeframes disagree")
        if (
            volume_pct is not None
            and volume_pct < 0
            and (three_min or one_min or 0) > 0
        ):
            risks.append("volume fading")
        if liquidity == "thin":
            risks.append("thin liquidity")
        elif liquidity == "wide":
            risks.append("wide spread")

        row = {
            "symbol": sym,
            "product_id": f"{sym}-USD",
            "live_score": score,
            "live_label": _label(score),
            "data_quality": data_quality,
            "observed_inputs": observed_inputs,
            "expected_inputs": expected_inputs,
            "live_reasons": reasons[:4],
            "live_risks": risks[:3],
            "live_components": {
                "momentum_1m": round(one_min_score, 1),
                "momentum_3m": round(three_min_score, 1),
                "trend_1h": round(one_hour_score, 1),
                "volume": round(volume_score, 1),
                "confirmation": round(confirmation_score, 1),
                "persistence": round(persistence_score, 1),
            },
            "model_version": LIVE_RANKING_MODEL_VERSION,
            "price": _finite(raw_row.get("price")),
        }
        if include_internal_features:
            # Matching inputs for the control benchmark. Every liquidity
            # candidate is recorded rather than one being chosen here: which is
            # usable depends on measured universe coverage and post-match
            # balance, and that selection is made by the dry-run, never inline
            # and never with outcomes in view.
            vol_row = volatilities.get(sym) or volatilities.get(f"{sym}-USD") or {}
            liq_row = liquidities.get(f"{sym}-USD") or liquidities.get(sym) or {}
            row["raw_inputs"] = {
                "pct_1m": one_min,
                "pct_3m": three_min,
                "pct_1h": one_hour,
                "volume_change_1h_pct": volume_pct,
                "trend_streak": streak,
                "bullish_alerts": counts["bullish"],
                "bearish_alerts": counts["bearish"],
                "spot_pressure": spot_pressure,
                "market_relation": market_relation,
                # Categorical liquidity bucket: universe-wide but coarse.
                "liquidity": liquidity,
                # Quantitative liquidity: precise but far narrower coverage.
                "quote_volume_usd": liq_row.get("quote_volume_usd"),
                "quote_volume_age_seconds": liq_row.get("age_seconds"),
                "quote_volume_minute_coverage": liq_row.get("minute_coverage"),
                "realized_volatility_pct_hour": vol_row.get("volatility_pct_hour"),
                "realized_volatility_observations": vol_row.get("observations"),
                "realized_volatility_staleness_seconds": vol_row.get(
                    "staleness_seconds"
                ),
                # None when volatility is available; a reason string otherwise,
                # so unavailable is never confused with low.
                "realized_volatility_unavailable_reason": vol_row.get("reason"),
                # Provenance of the categorical inputs is separate from their
                # legacy scoring defaults. Never infer measurement from a
                # default "normal"/"neutral" string in a retained snapshot.
                "context_input_supplied": {
                    key: context_row.get(key) is not None
                    for key in ("liquidity", "spot_pressure", "market_relation")
                },
                "quote_volume_hour_ts": liq_row.get("hour_ts"),
            }
            row["scoring_detail"] = {
                "weighted_before_penalty": round(weighted, 4),
                "chase_penalty": round(chase_penalty, 4),
                "raw_score": round(float(raw_score), 4),
                "reliability": round(reliability, 6),
            }
        rankings.append(row)

    rankings.sort(
        key=lambda row: (
            -row["live_score"],
            -row["data_quality"],
            row["symbol"],
        )
    )
    universe_size = len(rankings)
    for index, row in enumerate(rankings, start=1):
        row["live_rank"] = index
        row["universe_size"] = universe_size
    return rankings
===== END SOURCE 10 =====

===== BEGIN SOURCE 11/16: scripts/scoring_experiment/live_movers_fresh_replication.py =====
"""Fresh-data replication of the frozen live-movers rankers.

Protocol: docs/research/LIVE_MOVERS_FRESH_REPLICATION_PROTOCOL_20261113.md

    python scripts/scoring_experiment/live_movers_fresh_replication.py verify   # outcome-blind; may be repeated
    python scripts/scoring_experiment/live_movers_fresh_replication.py test     # once

verify  checks the frozen inputs, refits B2/M1 on the frozen train+discovery rows and requires the stored
        coefficients, copies the local ledger to a private capture and reports counts only.
test    scores the post-freeze window once with the unchanged models and applies the predeclared rule.

No parameter is fitted or selected on the fresh rows. The locked validation rows are never scored here.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import collections
import copy
import importlib.util
import json
import sqlite3
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
OUT = ROOT / "docs/research/results/live_movers_fresh_replication_20261113"
WORK = Path.home() / ".moonwalking/analysis/live_movers_fresh_replication_20261113"
SOURCE = Path.home() / ".moonwalking/data/signal_outcomes.sqlite"
FROZEN_RESULT = ROOT / "docs/research/results/live_movers_comparison/results_test_live_movers.json"
PROTOCOL = ROOT / "docs/research/LIVE_MOVERS_FRESH_REPLICATION_PROTOCOL_20261113.md"

PHASES = {"secondary_gap": ["2026-10-18T00:00:00+00:00", "2026-10-28T00:00:00+00:00"],
          "test": ["2026-10-28T00:00:00+00:00", "2026-11-13T00:00:00+00:00"]}
CAPTURE_END = "2026-11-13T00:00:00+00:00"
PRIVATE_PATH = "~/.moonwalking/analysis/live_movers_fresh_replication_20261113/board_episodes.sqlite"  # home-relative; outside Git
PRIMARY = ("confirmation_3m_up/up", "h5")
SEED, REPS = 20261113, 5000
MIN_EFFECT, MIN_DAYS = 0.02, 12
PAIRS = (("B2", "B1"), ("B2", "B0"), ("M1", "B2"), ("M1", "B1"))


def load(name: str):
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name, ROOT / f"scripts/scoring_experiment/{name}.py")
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
    return mod


def fresh_config(cfg: dict) -> dict:
    """The frozen contract with only the date windows replaced."""
    out = copy.deepcopy(cfg)
    out["phases"] = copy.deepcopy(PHASES)
    return out


def frozen_models(run, freeze, cfg: dict) -> tuple[dict, dict]:
    """Refit on the frozen train+discovery rows with the frozen alphas; refuse unless stored coefficients reproduce."""
    stored = json.loads(FROZEN_RESULT.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    df = run.load_episodes(run.CAPTURE, cfg, freeze)
    models, report = {}, {}
    for sname, (board, direction) in run.STRATA.items():
        s = df[(df["board"] == board) & (df["direction"] == direction)]
        fit_rows = s[s["phase"].isin(["train", "discovery"])]
        for hname, label in run.LABELS.items():
            cell = stored["strata"][sname][hname]
            got = {}
            for name, nonlinear in (("B2", False), ("M1", True)):
                m = run.fit_ridge(fit_rows, label, cell["alpha"][name], nonlinear)
                models[(sname, hname, name)] = m
                got[name] = [round(float(b), 6) for b in m["beta"]]
                if got[name] != list(cell["test"]["coefficients"][name].values()):
                    raise SystemExit(f"Refusing: refitted {name} coefficients differ from the frozen result in {sname}/{hname}.")
            report[f"{sname}/{hname}"] = {"fit_rows": int(len(fit_rows)), "alpha": cell["alpha"], "coefficients_match": True}
    return models, report


def capture_ledger(source: Path, work: Path, freeze) -> dict:
    """Copy board rows before the capture end from the live ledger, read-only, into a private file. Never overwrites."""
    db = work / "board_episodes.sqlite"
    if db.exists():
        return {"path": PRIVATE_PATH, "sha256": run_digest(db), "reused_existing_capture": True}
    work.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    src = sqlite3.connect(source.as_uri() + "?mode=ro", uri=True, timeout=30)
    src.execute("pragma query_only=on")
    src.execute("begin")
    cols = src.execute("pragma table_info(board_outcomes_v2)").fetchall()
    names = [c[1] for c in cols]
    quoted = lambda s: '"' + s.replace('"', '""') + '"'
    defs = [quoted(c[1]) + " " + (c[2] if c[2] in {"INTEGER", "REAL", "TEXT", "BLOB", "NUMERIC"} else "TEXT") for c in cols]
    dest = sqlite3.connect(db)
    dest.execute("create table episodes (" + ",".join(defs) + ")")
    cur = src.execute("select * from board_outcomes_v2 where entered_ts < ? order by id", (freeze.stamp(CAPTURE_END),))
    rows = 0
    with dest:
        while batch := cur.fetchmany(1000):
            dest.executemany("insert into episodes values (" + ",".join("?" for _ in names) + ")", batch)
            rows += len(batch)
    src.rollback()
    src.close()
    dest.execute("create unique index episode_id on episodes(id)")
    dest.execute("create index episode_time on episodes(entered_ts,id)")
    dest.commit()
    assert dest.execute("pragma integrity_check").fetchone()[0] == "ok"
    dest.close()
    return {"path": PRIVATE_PATH, "sha256": run_digest(db), "rows": rows, "reused_existing_capture": False,
            "captured_at_utc": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(), "capture_end_exclusive_utc": CAPTURE_END}


def run_digest(path: Path) -> str:
    return load("live_movers_run").digest(path)


def load_fresh(db: Path, cfg: dict, freeze, run) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, dict]:
    """Frozen eligibility over the whole local ledger in order, so cooldown state is exact. Returns eligible window rows."""
    con = sqlite3.connect(db.as_uri() + "?mode=ro", uri=True)
    con.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    cooldown, recs, rejects = {}, [], collections.Counter()
    for row in con.execute("select * from episodes order by entered_ts,id"):
        phase, why, snap = freeze.classify(row, cfg, cooldown)
        if why:
            rejects[why] += 1
            continue
        raw = snap["raw_inputs"]
        recs.append((row["id"], row["board"], row["product_id"], row["direction"], row["entered_ts"], phase,
                     run.num(raw["pct_1m"]), run.num(raw["pct_3m"]), run.num(raw["volume_change_1h_pct"]),
                     run.num(snap["live_score"]), run.num(row["start_change_pct"]),
                     run.num(row["gross_return_5m"]), run.num(row["gross_return_15m"])))
    con.close()
    df = pd.DataFrame(recs, columns=["id", "board", "product_id", "direction", "entered_ts", "phase", "pct_1m", "pct_3m",
                                     "vol", "live_score", "start_change_pct", "gross_return_5m", "gross_return_15m"])
    if df["id"].duplicated().any():
        raise SystemExit("Refusing: duplicate episode ids.")
    df["stratum"] = df["board"] + "/" + df["direction"]
    df["day"] = pd.to_datetime(df["entered_ts"], unit="s", utc=True).dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    return df, dict(rejects)


def coverage(df: pd.DataFrame) -> dict:
    """Counts only."""
    out = {}
    for phase in PHASES:
        p = df[df["phase"] == phase]
        out[phase] = {"rows": int(len(p)), "days": int(p["day"].nunique()), "products": int(p["product_id"].nunique()),
                      "rows_by_stratum_and_day": {s: g.groupby("day").size().astype(int).to_dict()
                                                  for s, g in p.groupby("stratum")}}
    return out


def interval(series: pd.Series, run) -> dict:
    s = series.dropna()
    return {"mean": float(s.mean()) if len(s) else None, "n_days": int(len(s)), "days_positive": int((s > 0).sum()),
            "iid_daily": run.boot_iid(s.to_numpy(), REPS, SEED), "moving_block_3d": run.boot_moving(s, REPS, SEED)}


def decide(primary: dict, b2: dict) -> str:
    """The predeclared rule. primary is the B2-B1 interval record; b2 is the B2 record."""
    iid, mov = primary["iid_daily"], primary["moving_block_3d"]
    if primary["n_days"] < MIN_DAYS or iid["lo"] is None or mov["lo"] is None:
        return "insufficient"
    if primary["mean"] <= 0 or (iid["hi"] < MIN_EFFECT and mov["hi"] < MIN_EFFECT):
        return "drop"
    if primary["mean"] >= MIN_EFFECT and iid["lo"] > 0 and mov["lo"] > 0 and b2["mean"] > 0:
        return "keep"
    return "inconclusive"


def score_cell(rows: pd.DataFrame, label: str, b2: dict, m1: dict, costs: list[float], run) -> dict:
    preds = {**run.baselines(rows), "B2": run.predict(b2, rows), "M1": run.predict(m1, rows)}
    ics = run.daily_ics(rows, preds, label)
    res = {"rows": int(len(rows)), "days_scored": int(len(ics)), "products": int(rows["product_id"].nunique()),
           "rows_on_scored_days": int(rows["day"].isin(ics.index).sum()),
           "daily_ic": ics.to_dict(orient="index"),
           "ic": {k: interval(ics[k], run) for k in run.MODELS} if len(ics) else {},
           "paired": {f"{a}-{b}": interval(ics[a] - ics[b], run) for a, b in PAIRS} if len(ics) else {},
           "bands": {}}
    for k in run.MODELS:
        bands = run.quintiles(rows, np.asarray(preds[k], dtype=float), label, costs) if len(rows) else []
        res["bands"][k] = {"by_rank_band": bands,
                           "top_minus_bottom_gross_pct": (bands[-1]["mean_gross_pct"] - bands[0]["mean_gross_pct"])
                           if len(bands) >= 2 else None}
    return res


def git_state(run) -> tuple[str, bool]:
    import subprocess
    head = subprocess.run(["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()
    dirty = bool(subprocess.run(["git", "status", "--porcelain", "--", "scripts", "docs/research"],
                                cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip())
    return head, dirty


def write_once(folder: Path, name: str, obj: dict, exclusive: bool) -> None:
    folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with (folder / name).open("x" if exclusive else "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as stream:
        stream.write(json.dumps(obj, indent=2, default=float, allow_nan=False) + "\n")


def main() -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    ap.add_argument("phase", choices=("verify", "test"))
    phase = ap.parse_args().phase
    run, freeze = load("live_movers_run"), load("freeze_live_contract")
    cfg = json.loads((run.SPEC_DIR / "CONTRACT_PRODUCTION.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    hashes = run.verify_inputs()
    head, dirty = git_state(run)
    code = {name: run.digest(ROOT / name) for name in
            ("scripts/scoring_experiment/live_movers_fresh_replication.py", "scripts/scoring_experiment/live_movers_run.py",
             "scripts/scoring_experiment/freeze_live_contract.py")}
    out = {"phase": phase, "protocol_sha256": run.digest(PROTOCOL), "code_commit": head, "code_dirty": dirty,
           "code_hashes": code, "runtime_versions": {"numpy": np.__version__, "pandas": pd.__version__},
           "frozen_inputs": hashes, "frozen_result_sha256": run.digest(FROZEN_RESULT), "windows_utc": PHASES,
           "seed": SEED, "bootstrap_replicates": REPS, "minimum_effect_ic": MIN_EFFECT, "minimum_scored_days": MIN_DAYS}
    if phase == "test":
        if dirty:
            raise SystemExit("Refusing: uncommitted script or research changes; commit first so the result names its code.")
        vpath = WORK / "results_verify_fresh_replication.json"
        if not vpath.exists():
            raise SystemExit("Refusing: run verify first.")
        ver = json.loads(vpath.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if ver["code_hashes"] != code or ver["protocol_sha256"] != out["protocol_sha256"]:
            raise SystemExit("Refusing: code or protocol changed since verify.")
        if any((f / "results_test_fresh_replication.json").exists() for f in (WORK, OUT)):
            raise SystemExit("Refusing: the fresh replication has already been run once.")
        try:
            write_once(WORK, "test_attempt_fresh_replication.json", {"code_commit": head, "code_hashes": code}, True)
        except FileExistsError:
            raise SystemExit("Refusing: an attempt already exists, including an interrupted run.")
    models, fit_report = frozen_models(run, freeze, cfg)
    out["frozen_model_reproduction"] = fit_report
    cap = capture_ledger(SOURCE, WORK, freeze)
    if phase == "test" and cap["sha256"] != ver["capture"]["sha256"]:
        raise SystemExit("Refusing: capture differs from the one verify recorded.")
    out["capture"] = cap
    df, rejects = load_fresh(WORK / "board_episodes.sqlite", fresh_config(cfg), freeze, run)
    out["rejections"], out["coverage"] = rejects, coverage(df)
    if phase == "verify":
        out["outcome_values_summarized"] = False
        write_once(WORK, "results_verify_fresh_replication.json", out, False)
        write_once(OUT, "results_verify_fresh_replication.json", out, False)
        print(json.dumps({"capture_rows": cap.get("rows"), "rejections": rejects,
                          **{p: {k: v for k, v in c.items() if k != "rows_by_stratum_and_day"}
                             for p, c in out["coverage"].items()}}, indent=1))
        return 0
    costs = cfg["cost_scenarios_pct_round_trip"]
    out["windows"] = {}
    for window in PHASES:
        out["windows"][window] = {}
        for sname in run.STRATA:
            rows = df[(df["phase"] == window) & (df["stratum"] == sname)]
            out["windows"][window][sname] = {
                hname: score_cell(rows, label, models[(sname, hname, "B2")], models[(sname, hname, "M1")], costs, run)
                for hname, label in run.LABELS.items()}
    cell = out["windows"]["test"][PRIMARY[0]][PRIMARY[1]]
    record = cell["paired"].get("B2-B1") or {"n_days": 0, "mean": None, "iid_daily": {"lo": None, "hi": None},
                                             "moving_block_3d": {"lo": None, "hi": None}}
    out["primary"] = {"window": "test", "stratum": PRIMARY[0], "label": run.LABELS[PRIMARY[1]],
                      "statistic": "mean daily IC(B2)-IC(B1)", **record,
                      "b2_mean_daily_ic": cell["ic"].get("B2", {}).get("mean"),
                      "verdict": decide(record, cell["ic"].get("B2", {"mean": None}))}
    write_once(WORK, "results_test_fresh_replication.json", out, True)
    write_once(OUT, "results_test_fresh_replication.json", out, True)
    p = out["primary"]
    print("primary B2-B1", {"mean": p["mean"], "iid": p["iid_daily"], "block": p["moving_block_3d"],
                            "n_days": p["n_days"], "B2": p["b2_mean_daily_ic"]}, "->", p["verdict"])
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
===== END SOURCE 11 =====

===== BEGIN SOURCE 12/16: scripts/scoring_experiment/live_movers_run.py =====
"""Live-movers short-horizon comparison (COMPARISON_SPEC.json, contract live-movers-contract-v1-production-amendment-1).

    python scripts/scoring_experiment/live_movers_run.py verify      # hashes, eligible-row identity, readiness; no outcomes
    python scripts/scoring_experiment/live_movers_run.py discovery   # alpha selection on discovery; freezes the choices
    python scripts/scoring_experiment/live_movers_run.py test        # once; refuses without a matching discovery artifact

B0 = |start_change_pct|; B1 = stored live_score (100 - score on the down board); B2 = ridge on direction-aligned pct_1m, pct_3m and
volume_change_1h_pct; M1 = B2 plus squares and interactions. Every model is scored on identical eligible episodes and identical
daily groups. Historical pilot on last-trade labels: not executable fills, not an untouched holdout, no live-weight change.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import hashlib
import importlib.util
import json
import sqlite3
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
SPEC_DIR = ROOT / "docs/research/results/live_movers_contract_20261027"
OUT = ROOT / "docs/research/results/live_movers_comparison"
CAPTURE = Path.home() / ".moonwalking/analysis/live_movers_contract_20261027_production"
WORK = Path.home() / ".moonwalking/analysis/live_movers_comparison"
STRATA = {"ignition_1m/up": ("ignition_1m", "up"), "confirmation_3m_up/up": ("confirmation_3m_up", "up"),
          "confirmation_3m_down/down": ("confirmation_3m_down", "down")}
LABELS = {"h5": "gross_return_5m", "h15": "gross_return_15m"}
MODELS = ("B0", "B1", "B2", "M1")
MIN_DAY_ROWS = 20


def digest(path: Path) -> str:
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as f:
        for block in iter(lambda: f.read(1 << 20), b""):
            h.update(block)
    return h.hexdigest()


def load_freeze():
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("freeze_live", ROOT / "scripts/scoring_experiment/freeze_live_contract.py")
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
    return mod


# ---------- inputs and identity ----------

def verify_inputs(capture: Path = CAPTURE) -> dict:
    """Refuse unless every frozen file, the contract and the spec are exactly the committed ones."""
    contract_p, spec_p = SPEC_DIR / "CONTRACT_PRODUCTION.json", SPEC_DIR / "COMPARISON_SPEC.json"
    manifest = json.loads((SPEC_DIR / "PRODUCTION_CAPTURE_MANIFEST.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    for name, rec in manifest["files"].items():
        got = digest(capture / name)
        if got != rec["sha256"]:
            raise SystemExit(f"Refusing: frozen capture file {name} hash differs from the committed manifest.")
    if digest(contract_p) != manifest["contract_sha256"] or digest(capture / "CONTRACT.json") != manifest["contract_sha256"]:
        raise SystemExit("Refusing: contract differs from the one the capture was frozen with.")
    if digest(ROOT / "scripts/scoring_experiment/freeze_live_contract.py") != manifest["freeze_script_sha256"]:
        raise SystemExit("Refusing: eligibility code differs from the frozen freeze script.")
    spec = json.loads(spec_p.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if spec.get("data_contract") != "CONTRACT_PRODUCTION.json":
        raise SystemExit("Refusing: comparison spec names a different data contract.")
    return {"capture_files": {k: v["sha256"] for k, v in manifest["files"].items()},
            "contract_sha256": manifest["contract_sha256"], "spec_sha256": digest(spec_p),
            "manifest_sha256": digest(SPEC_DIR / "PRODUCTION_CAPTURE_MANIFEST.json")}


def num(x):
    return float(x) if isinstance(x, (int, float)) and not isinstance(x, bool) and np.isfinite(x) else np.nan


def load_episodes(capture: Path, cfg: dict, freeze) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Re-derive eligibility and require every frozen identity, timestamp and phase in order."""
    con = sqlite3.connect((capture / "board_episodes.sqlite").as_uri() + "?mode=ro", uri=True)
    con.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    cooldown, recs = {}, []
    for row in con.execute("select * from episodes order by entered_ts,id"):
        phase, why, snap = freeze.classify(row, cfg, cooldown)
        if why:
            continue
        raw = snap["raw_inputs"]
        recs.append((row["id"], row["board"], row["product_id"], row["direction"], row["entered_ts"], phase,
                     num(raw["pct_1m"]), num(raw["pct_3m"]), num(raw["volume_change_1h_pct"]), num(snap["live_score"]),
                     num(row["start_change_pct"]), num(row["gross_return_5m"]), num(row["gross_return_15m"])))
    con.close()
    df = pd.DataFrame(recs, columns=["id", "board", "product_id", "direction", "entered_ts", "phase", "pct_1m", "pct_3m",
                                     "vol", "live_score", "start_change_pct", "gross_return_5m", "gross_return_15m"])
    with (capture / "eligible_episodes.csv").open(encoding="utf-8", newline="") as f:
        frozen = [(int(r["id"]), r["board"], r["product_id"], r["direction"], int(r["entered_ts"]), r["phase"])
                  for r in csv.DictReader(f)]
    if list(df[["id", "board", "product_id", "direction", "entered_ts", "phase"]].itertuples(index=False, name=None)) != frozen:
        raise SystemExit("Refusing: re-derived eligible episodes differ from the frozen eligible list.")
    if df["id"].duplicated().any():
        raise SystemExit("Refusing: duplicate episode ids.")
    df["stratum"] = df["board"] + "/" + df["direction"]
    df["day"] = pd.to_datetime(df["entered_ts"], unit="s", utc=True).dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    return df


def readiness(df: pd.DataFrame, cfg: dict) -> dict:
    out = {}
    for ph, req in cfg["readiness"].items():
        s = df[df["phase"] == ph]
        r = {"rows": int(len(s)), "days": int(s["day"].nunique()), "products": int(s["product_id"].nunique())}
        r["pass"] = r["days"] >= req["min_days"] and r["rows"] >= req["min_episodes"] and r["products"] >= req["min_products"]
        out[ph] = r
    return out


# ---------- models ----------

def aligned(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    sign = np.where(df["direction"] == "down", -1.0, 1.0)
    return pd.DataFrame({"a1": sign * df["pct_1m"], "a3": sign * df["pct_3m"], "v": df["vol"]}, index=df.index)


class Design:
    """1/99 winsor limits and standardization fitted on fitting rows only; transforms from clipped raw inputs."""

    def __init__(self, nonlinear: bool):
        self.nonlinear = nonlinear

    def _cols(self, r: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
        c = r.clip(self.lo, self.hi, axis=1)
        X = c.copy()
        if self.nonlinear:
            X["a1_sq"], X["a3_sq"] = c["a1"] ** 2, c["a3"] ** 2
            X["a1_a3"], X["a1_v"], X["a3_v"] = c["a1"] * c["a3"], c["a1"] * c["v"], c["a3"] * c["v"]
        return X

    def fit(self, r: pd.DataFrame) -> "Design":
        self.lo, self.hi = r.quantile(0.01), r.quantile(0.99)
        X = self._cols(r)
        self.mu, self.sd = X.mean(), X.std(ddof=0).replace(0, 1.0)
        return self

    def transform(self, r: pd.DataFrame) -> np.ndarray:
        return ((self._cols(r) - self.mu) / self.sd).to_numpy()


def fit_ridge(df: pd.DataFrame, label: str, alpha: float, nonlinear: bool):
    d = Design(nonlinear).fit(aligned(df))
    X, y = d.transform(aligned(df)), df[label].to_numpy()
    beta = np.linalg.solve(X.T @ X + alpha * np.eye(X.shape[1]), X.T @ (y - y.mean()))
    return {"design": d, "beta": beta, "intercept": float(y.mean()), "alpha": alpha, "nonlinear": nonlinear}


def predict(model: dict, df: pd.DataFrame) -> np.ndarray:
    return model["design"].transform(aligned(df)) @ model["beta"] + model["intercept"]


def baselines(df: pd.DataFrame) -> dict:
    b1 = np.where(df["direction"] == "down", 100.0 - df["live_score"], df["live_score"])
    return {"B0": df["start_change_pct"].abs().to_numpy(), "B1": b1}


# ---------- statistics ----------

def daily_ics(df: pd.DataFrame, preds: dict, label: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Within-day Spearman IC per model on identical day groups: >=20 rows, nonconstant target and every prediction."""
    frame = pd.DataFrame({k: v for k, v in preds.items()}, index=df.index)
    frame["_y"], frame["_day"] = df[label].to_numpy(), df["day"].to_numpy()
    rows = {}
    for day, g in frame.groupby("_day"):
        if len(g) < MIN_DAY_ROWS or g["_y"].nunique() < 2 or any(g[k].nunique() < 2 for k in preds):
            continue
        ry = g["_y"].rank()
        rows[day] = {k: float(np.corrcoef(g[k].rank(), ry)[0, 1]) for k in preds}
    return pd.DataFrame.from_dict(rows, orient="index").sort_index()


def boot_iid(v: np.ndarray, reps: int, seed: int, level: float = 0.95) -> dict:
    n = len(v)
    out = {"n_days": n, "mean": float(v.mean()) if n else None, "lo": None, "hi": None}
    if n < 3:
        return out
    m = v[np.random.default_rng(seed).integers(0, n, (reps, n))].mean(axis=1)
    a = (1 - level) / 2 * 100
    out["lo"], out["hi"] = (float(x) for x in np.percentile(m, [a, 100 - a]))
    return out


def boot_moving(s: pd.Series, reps: int, seed: int, block: int = 3, level: float = 0.95) -> dict:
    """Blocks of `block` consecutive UTC calendar days; missing days stay missing (contribute nothing), gaps are not bridged."""
    s = s.dropna()
    out = {"n_days": int(len(s)), "block_days": block, "mean": float(s.mean()) if len(s) else None, "lo": None, "hi": None}
    if len(s) < 3 * block:
        return out
    cal = pd.date_range(s.index.min(), s.index.max(), freq="D").strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    vals = s.reindex(cal).to_numpy()
    k = int(np.ceil(len(cal) / block))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    means = np.empty(reps)
    starts = rng.integers(0, len(cal) - block + 1, (reps, k))
    for i in range(reps):
        w = vals[(starts[i][:, None] + np.arange(block)).ravel()]
        means[i] = np.nanmean(w)
    a = (1 - level) / 2 * 100
    out["lo"], out["hi"] = (float(x) for x in np.percentile(means, [a, 100 - a]))
    return out


def paired(ics: pd.DataFrame, a: str, b: str, spec: dict, offset: int = 0) -> dict:
    d = (ics[a] - ics[b]).dropna()
    # The frozen specification fixes the seed for every reported interval.
    # Keep offset in the call signature for compatibility; it must not alter it.
    reps, seed = 5000, 20261027
    return {"difference": f"{a}-{b}", "bootstrap_replicates": reps, "bootstrap_seed": seed,
            "iid_daily": boot_iid(d.to_numpy(), reps, seed),
            "moving_block_3d": boot_moving(d, reps, seed)}


def verdict(primary: dict, discovery_mean: float | None, mei: float) -> str:
    iid, mov = primary["iid_daily"], primary["moving_block_3d"]
    if iid["lo"] is None:
        return "inconclusive"
    if iid["hi"] < mei:
        return "below the minimum effect"
    if (iid["mean"] >= mei and iid["lo"] > 0 and mov["lo"] is not None and mov["lo"] > 0
            and discovery_mean is not None and discovery_mean > 0):
        return "historically supportive"
    return "inconclusive"


def to_score(pred: np.ndarray, reference: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """0-100 percentile against the fixed train+discovery prediction distribution. Ordinal; not a probability."""
    ref = np.sort(reference)
    return 100.0 * np.searchsorted(ref, pred, side="right") / len(ref)


def quintiles(df: pd.DataFrame, score: np.ndarray, label: str, costs: list[float]) -> list[dict]:
    # Average-rank percentile bands keep identical scores together. Empty bands
    # are omitted; a constant score has one band, never an arbitrary time split.
    ranks = pd.Series(score, index=df.index).rank(method="average")
    q = np.minimum(4, np.floor(5 * (ranks - 0.5) / len(df))).astype(int) + 1
    out = []
    for k in sorted(q.unique()):
        g = df.loc[q == k, label]
        out.append({"quintile": int(k), "n": int(len(g)), "mean_gross_pct": float(g.mean()),
                    **{f"mean_gross_minus_{c}pct": float(g.mean() - c) for c in costs}})
    return out


# ---------- phases ----------

def select_alphas(train: pd.DataFrame, disc: pd.DataFrame, label: str, alphas: list[float]) -> dict:
    """Fit on train only; choose alpha by discovery mean daily IC; ties -> larger alpha."""
    chosen, trace = {}, {}
    base = baselines(disc)
    for name, nl in (("B2", False), ("M1", True)):
        best, best_ic = None, -np.inf
        trace[name] = {}
        for a in sorted(alphas, reverse=True):
            p = predict(fit_ridge(train, label, a, nl), disc)
            ics = daily_ics(disc, {**base, "B2" if name == "B2" else "M1": p}, label)
            m = float(ics[name].mean()) if len(ics) else -np.inf
            trace[name][str(a)] = m
            if m > best_ic:
                best, best_ic = a, m
        chosen[name] = best
    return {"alpha": chosen, "discovery_ic_by_alpha": trace}


def evaluate(fit_rows: pd.DataFrame, eval_rows: pd.DataFrame, label: str, alpha: dict, costs: list[float], spec: dict,
             offset: int) -> dict:
    models = {"B2": fit_ridge(fit_rows, label, alpha["B2"], False), "M1": fit_ridge(fit_rows, label, alpha["M1"], True)}
    preds = {**baselines(eval_rows), **{k: predict(m, eval_rows) for k, m in models.items()}}
    ics = daily_ics(eval_rows, preds, label)
    res = {"rows": int(len(eval_rows)), "days_scored": int(len(ics)), "products": int(eval_rows["product_id"].nunique()),
           "rows_per_day": eval_rows.groupby("day").size().astype(int).to_dict(),
           "daily_ic": ics.to_dict(orient="index"),
           "rows_on_scored_days": int(eval_rows["day"].isin(ics.index).sum()),
           "excluded_day_groups": {day: {"rows": int(len(g)), "below_minimum_rows": len(g) < MIN_DAY_ROWS,
                                         "constant_target": g[label].nunique() < 2,
                                         "constant_predictions": [k for k, p in preds.items()
                                                                  if pd.Series(p, index=eval_rows.index).loc[g.index].nunique() < 2]}
                                   for day, g in eval_rows.groupby("day") if day not in ics.index},
           "quintile_policy": "Average-rank percentile bands; equal scores stay together; empty bands omitted.",
           "mean_daily_ic": {k: float(ics[k].mean()) if len(ics) else None for k in MODELS},
           "paired": {f"{a}-{b}": paired(ics, a, b, spec, offset + i) for i, (a, b) in
                      enumerate((("M1", "B2"), ("M1", "B1"), ("B2", "B1"), ("B2", "B0"), ("M1", "B0")))},
           "coefficients": {k: dict(zip(["a1", "a3", "v"] + (["a1_sq", "a3_sq", "a1_a3", "a1_v", "a3_v"] if m["nonlinear"] else []),
                                        [round(float(b), 6) for b in m["beta"]])) for k, m in models.items()}}
    ref = {k: predict(m, fit_rows) for k, m in models.items()}
    res["display_score"] = {}
    for k in ("B2", "M1"):
        sc = to_score(preds[k], ref[k])
        res["display_score"][k] = {"share_at_0": float((sc == 0).mean()), "share_at_100": float((sc == 100).mean()),
                                   "quintiles_by_score": quintiles(eval_rows, sc, label, costs)}
    for k in ("B0", "B1"):
        res["display_score"][k] = {"quintiles_by_score": quintiles(eval_rows, preds[k], label, costs)}
    return res


def git_state() -> tuple[str, bool]:
    head = subprocess.run(["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()
    dirty = bool(subprocess.run(["git", "status", "--porcelain", "--", "scripts", "docs/research/results/live_movers_contract_20261027"],
                                cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip())
    return head, dirty


def code_fingerprint() -> dict:
    return {name: digest(ROOT / name) for name in
            ("scripts/scoring_experiment/live_movers_run.py", "scripts/scoring_experiment/freeze_live_contract.py")}


def require_discovery(path: Path, hashes: dict, head: str, code_hashes: dict) -> dict:
    if not path.exists():
        raise SystemExit("Refusing to run the test: no discovery artifact.")
    disc = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if disc.get("phase") != "discovery" or disc.get("inputs") != hashes:
        raise SystemExit("Refusing to run the test: inputs differ from the discovery run.")
    if disc.get("code_hashes") != code_hashes:
        raise SystemExit("Refusing to run the test: code hashes changed or are missing since the discovery run.")
    return disc


def reserve_test(work: Path, out: Path, metadata: dict) -> None:
    """Refuse portable/local results and atomically reserve even an interrupted attempt."""
    if any((folder / "results_test_live_movers.json").exists() for folder in (work, out)):
        raise SystemExit("Refusing: the historical validation has already been run once.")
    work.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    try:
        with (work / "test_attempt_live_movers.json").open("x", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            json.dump(metadata, stream, indent=2, allow_nan=False)
    except FileExistsError:
        raise SystemExit("Refusing: a historical validation attempt already exists, including an interrupted run.")


def main() -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("phase", choices=("verify", "discovery", "test"))
    phase = ap.parse_args().phase
    cfg = json.loads((SPEC_DIR / "CONTRACT_PRODUCTION.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    spec = json.loads((SPEC_DIR / "COMPARISON_SPEC.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    hashes = verify_inputs()
    code_hashes = code_fingerprint()
    head, dirty = git_state()
    if dirty and phase != "verify":
        raise SystemExit("Refusing: uncommitted driver or contract changes; commit first so the result names its code.")
    df = load_episodes(CAPTURE, cfg, load_freeze())
    ready = readiness(df, cfg)
    out = {"phase": phase, "spec_version": spec["version"], "contract_version": cfg["version"], "code_commit": head,
           "code_hashes": code_hashes, "code_dirty": dirty, "runtime_versions": {"numpy": np.__version__, "pandas": pd.__version__},
           "inputs": hashes, "readiness": ready, "eligible_rows": int(len(df)),
           "limits": [cfg["retrospective_scope"], cfg["historical_holdout_limit"], spec["live_release"],
                      "Scores are ordinal 0-100 percentiles, direction- and horizon-labelled; not probabilities.",
                      "pct_1h/trend_streak complete-case secondary not run."]}
    if not all(r["pass"] for r in ready.values()):
        raise SystemExit(f"Refusing: readiness fails {ready}")
    if phase == "verify":
        _write(out, phase)
        print(json.dumps(ready, indent=1))
        return 0
    dpath = WORK / "results_discovery_live_movers.json"
    costs, mei = cfg["cost_scenarios_pct_round_trip"], spec["minimum_effect_of_interest_ic"]
    out["strata"] = {}
    if phase == "test":
        # A committed discovery artifact is portable; a docs/result-only commit
        # must not require refitting or invalidate unchanged code and inputs.
        if not dpath.exists():
            dpath = OUT / "results_discovery_live_movers.json"
        disc = require_discovery(dpath, hashes, head, code_hashes)
        reserve_test(WORK, OUT, {"code_commit": head, "code_hashes": code_hashes, "inputs": hashes})
    for si, (sname, (board, direction)) in enumerate(STRATA.items()):
        s = df[(df["board"] == board) & (df["direction"] == direction)]
        tr, dc, te = (s[s["phase"] == p] for p in ("train", "discovery", "test"))
        out["strata"][sname] = {}
        for hi, (hname, label) in enumerate(LABELS.items()):
            off = 100 * si + 10 * hi
            if phase == "discovery":
                sel = select_alphas(tr, dc, label, spec["ridge_alphas"])
                r = {"selection": sel, "discovery": evaluate(tr, dc, label, sel["alpha"], costs, spec, off)}
            else:
                dsel = disc["strata"][sname][hname]
                alpha = dsel["selection"]["alpha"]
                r = {"alpha": alpha, "test": evaluate(pd.concat([tr, dc]), te, label, alpha, costs, spec, off)}
                if sname == spec["primary_stratum"] and label == spec["primary_label"]:
                    dmean = dsel["discovery"]["paired"]["M1-B2"]["iid_daily"]["mean"]
                    out["primary"] = {"stratum": sname, "label": label, "statistic": "mean daily IC(M1)-IC(B2)",
                                      **r["test"]["paired"]["M1-B2"], "discovery_difference_mean": dmean,
                                      "minimum_effect_of_interest_ic": mei,
                                      "verdict": verdict(r["test"]["paired"]["M1-B2"], dmean, mei)}
            out["strata"][sname][hname] = r
    _write(out, phase)
    if phase == "test":
        p = out["primary"]["iid_daily"]
        print("test", spec["primary_stratum"], "M1-B2", {k: None if p[k] is None else round(p[k], 4) for k in ("mean", "lo", "hi")},
              out["primary"]["verdict"])
    else:
        print("discovery alphas:", {s: {h: v["selection"]["alpha"] for h, v in hs.items()} for s, hs in out["strata"].items()})
    return 0


def _write(out: dict, phase: str) -> None:
    WORK.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    text = json.dumps(out, indent=2, default=float, allow_nan=False)
    for folder in (WORK, OUT):
        with (folder / f"results_{phase}_live_movers.json").open("x" if phase == "test" else "w", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            stream.write(text + "\n")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

===== BEGIN SOURCE 13/16: scripts/scoring_experiment/freeze_live_contract.py =====
"""Freeze one consistent board ledger and outcome-blind eligibility metadata.

Reads the live SQLite database with mode=ro and an explicit read transaction.
Copies episode rows byte-for-byte at the value level to a separate research DB.
Only feature/label presence, never outcome magnitudes, informs the report.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import collections
import argparse
import csv
import hashlib
import json
import math
import os
import re
import sqlite3
import stat
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
OUT = ROOT / "docs/research/results/live_movers_contract_20261027"
RAW = Path.home() / ".moonwalking/analysis/live_movers_contract_20261027"
SOURCE = Path.home() / ".moonwalking/data/signal_outcomes.sqlite"


def digest(path):
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as f:
        for block in iter(lambda: f.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            h.update(block)
    return h.hexdigest()


def write(path, obj):
    path.write_text(json.dumps(obj, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")


def stamp(text):
    return int(datetime.fromisoformat(text).timestamp())


def finite(value):
    return isinstance(value, (int, float)) and not isinstance(value, bool) and math.isfinite(value)


def phase_at(ts, cfg):
    for phase, (start, end) in cfg["phases"].items():
        upper = stamp(end) - (cfg["purge_before_next_phase_seconds"] if phase != "test" else 0)
        if stamp(start) <= ts < upper:
            return phase
    return None


def classify(row, cfg, cooldown):
    pid, board, ts = row["product_id"], row["board"], row["entered_ts"]
    if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Z0-9][A-Z0-9.\-]*-USD", pid or ""):
        return None, "noncanonical_identity", None
    if board not in {"ignition_1m", "confirmation_3m_up", "confirmation_3m_down"} or row["direction"] not in {"up", "down"}:
        return None, "invalid_board_or_direction", None
    key = (board, pid)
    if ts - cooldown.get(key, -10**12) < cfg["rolling_episode_cooldown_seconds"]:
        return None, "rolling_cooldown", None
    # Keep the first episode before inspecting its feature completeness.
    cooldown[key] = ts
    phase = phase_at(ts, cfg)
    if phase is None:
        return None, "outside_phases_or_purged", None
    try:
        snap = json.loads(row["feature_snapshot_json"] or "null")
    except ValueError:
        return None, "invalid_snapshot", None
    if not isinstance(snap, dict):
        return None, "missing_snapshot", None
    if row["feature_schema_version"] != cfg["required_snapshot_version"] or snap.get("model_version") != cfg["required_model_version"]:
        return None, "snapshot_or_model_version", snap
    captured = snap.get("captured_ts")
    if not finite(captured) or not 0 <= ts-captured <= cfg["max_snapshot_age_seconds"]:
        return None, "snapshot_timestamp", snap
    raw = snap.get("raw_inputs") or {}
    if not isinstance(raw, dict) or not all(finite(raw.get(k)) for k in cfg["required_raw_features"]):
        return None, "required_features_missing", snap
    if raw["pct_1m"] <= -100 or raw["pct_3m"] <= -100 or not finite(row["start_price"]) or row["start_price"] <= 0:
        return None, "invalid_price_input", snap
    if not finite(snap.get("live_score")):
        return None, "baseline_score_missing", snap
    if not all(finite(row[k]) for k in cfg["required_labels"]):
        return None, "label_unavailable", snap
    return phase, None, snap


def profile(db, cfg):
    con = sqlite3.connect(db.as_uri() + "?mode=ro", uri=True)
    con.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    cooldown, rejects = {}, collections.Counter()
    phases = {k: {"rows": 0, "days": set(), "products": set(), "strata": collections.Counter()} for k in cfg["phases"]}
    daily = collections.defaultdict(lambda: {"rows": 0, "with_snapshot": 0, "non_null": collections.Counter()})
    fields = cfg["required_raw_features"] + cfg["optional_primary_features"] + cfg["forward_only_features"]
    with (RAW / "eligible_episodes.csv").open("w", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as f:
        writer = csv.writer(f, lineterminator="\n")
        writer.writerow(["id", "board", "product_id", "direction", "entered_ts", "phase"])
        for row in con.execute("select * from episodes order by entered_ts,id"):
            day = datetime.fromtimestamp(row["entered_ts"], timezone.utc).date().isoformat()
            d = daily[day]
            d["rows"] += 1
            try:
                s = json.loads(row["feature_snapshot_json"] or "null")
            except ValueError:
                s = None
            if isinstance(s, dict):
                d["with_snapshot"] += 1
                raw = s.get("raw_inputs") or {}
                for k in fields:
                    if finite(raw.get(k)):
                        d["non_null"][k] += 1
            phase, why, _ = classify(row, cfg, cooldown)
            if why:
                rejects[why] += 1
                continue
            p = phases[phase]
            p["rows"] += 1
            p["days"].add(day)
            p["products"].add(row["product_id"])
            p["strata"][row["board"] + "/" + row["direction"]] += 1
            writer.writerow([row[k] for k in ["id", "board", "product_id", "direction", "entered_ts"]] + [phase])
    con.close()
    for name, p in phases.items():
        p["days"] = len(p["days"])
        p["products"] = len(p["products"])
        req = cfg["readiness"][name]
        p["pass"] = p["days"] >= req["min_days"] and p["rows"] >= req["min_episodes"] and p["products"] >= req["min_products"]
    return {"phases": phases, "rejections": dict(rejects), "feature_coverage_by_day": dict(sorted(daily.items())),
            "ready": all(p["pass"] for p in phases.values()), "outcome_values_summarized": False}


def main():
    global RAW, SOURCE
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--source", type=Path, default=SOURCE)
    parser.add_argument("--capture-dir", type=Path, default=RAW)
    parser.add_argument("--contract", type=Path, default=OUT / "CONTRACT.json")
    parser.add_argument("--report-prefix", default="")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    RAW, SOURCE = args.capture_dir.resolve(), args.source.resolve()
    cfg_path = args.contract.resolve()
    if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Z_]*", args.report_prefix):
        raise SystemExit("Report prefix must contain only uppercase letters and underscores")
    cfg = json.loads(cfg_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if RAW.exists():
        raise SystemExit("Research capture already exists; do not overwrite the frozen snapshot")
    RAW.mkdir(parents=True)
    source = sqlite3.connect(SOURCE.as_uri() + "?mode=ro", uri=True, timeout=5)
    source.execute("pragma query_only=on")
    source.execute("begin")
    cols = source.execute("pragma table_info(board_outcomes_v2)").fetchall()
    db = RAW / "board_episodes.sqlite"
    dest = sqlite3.connect(db)
    names = [c[1] for c in cols]
    quoted = lambda s: '"' + s.replace('"', '""') + '"'
    definitions = [quoted(c[1]) + " " + (c[2] if c[2] in {"INTEGER", "REAL", "TEXT", "BLOB", "NUMERIC"} else "TEXT") for c in cols]
    dest.execute("create table episodes (" + ",".join(definitions) + ")")
    cur = source.execute("select * from board_outcomes_v2 where entered_ts < ? order by id", (stamp(cfg["decision_cutoff_exclusive_utc"]),))
    rows = 0
    with dest:
        while True:
            batch = cur.fetchmany(1000)
            if not batch:
                break
            dest.executemany("insert into episodes values (" + ",".join("?" for _ in names) + ")", batch)
            rows += len(batch)
    source.rollback()
    source.close()
    dest.execute("create unique index episode_id on episodes(id)")
    dest.execute("create index episode_time on episodes(entered_ts,id)")
    dest.commit()
    assert dest.execute("pragma integrity_check").fetchone()[0] == "ok"
    dest.close()
    result = profile(db, cfg)
    write(RAW / "CONTRACT.json", cfg)
    manifest = {"captured_at_utc": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(), "source": str(SOURCE),
                "source_access": "mode=ro; explicit consistent read transaction; rows copied without revision",
                "source_table": "board_outcomes_v2", "rows": rows,
                "decision_cutoff_exclusive_utc": cfg["decision_cutoff_exclusive_utc"],
                "contract_sha256": digest(cfg_path), "freeze_script_sha256": digest(Path(__file__)),
                "files": {p.name: {"bytes": p.stat().st_size, "sha256": digest(p)} for p in RAW.iterdir() if p.is_file()}}
    write(RAW / "MANIFEST.json", manifest)
    result.update(rows_captured=rows, manifest_sha256=digest(RAW / "MANIFEST.json"))
    write(OUT / (args.report_prefix + "COVERAGE.json"), result)
    write(OUT / (args.report_prefix + "CAPTURE_MANIFEST.json"), manifest)
    for p in RAW.iterdir():
        if p.is_file():
            os.chmod(p, stat.S_IREAD)
    print(json.dumps({"rows_captured": rows, "phases": result["phases"], "ready": result["ready"]}, indent=2))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== BEGIN SOURCE 14/16: backend/candle_archive.py =====
"""Exchange candle archive for periods the collector did not see.

This is raw Coinbase Exchange market data, not a replay of Moonwalking's own
signals or boards. It lives in its own SQLite file (MW_CANDLE_ARCHIVE_DB) that
no live code path reads, so it can never be mixed silently with live
collection; the file's ``archive_meta`` table and every fetched window record
the source and methodology that produced the rows.

What it is good for: the price and volume tape for any minute, including the
minutes the live stores have already pruned (price snapshots keep 24h, minute
volume 48h). That is what you need to recompute a return checkpoint the live
tracker missed, or to add a matched-peer control to an old signal.

What it cannot restore: anything only the live app saw, such as which alerts
fired, board membership as ranked live, order-book state, or social inputs.

Coinbase returns no candle for a minute with no trades, so an absent minute
inside a fetched window means "no trades", not "missing data". The
``fetched_windows`` table is the record of what was actually fetched.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import os
import sqlite3
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Iterable, Iterator

import requests

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

SOURCE = "coinbase-exchange-candles"
METHODOLOGY_VERSION = "exchange-candles-v1"
CANDLES_URL = "https://api.exchange.coinbase.com/products/{product_id}/candles"
PRODUCTS_URL = "https://api.exchange.coinbase.com/products"
MAX_CANDLES_PER_REQUEST = 300
GRANULARITIES = (60, 300, 900, 3600, 21600, 86400)

SCHEMA = """
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS archive_meta (
    key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    value TEXT NOT NULL
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS candles (
    product_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    granularity INTEGER NOT NULL,   -- seconds per candle
    ts INTEGER NOT NULL,            -- candle open time, unix seconds (UTC)
    open REAL NOT NULL,
    high REAL NOT NULL,
    low REAL NOT NULL,
    close REAL NOT NULL,
    volume REAL NOT NULL,           -- base-currency volume
    PRIMARY KEY (product_id, granularity, ts)
) WITHOUT ROWID;
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS fetched_windows (
    product_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    granularity INTEGER NOT NULL,
    window_start INTEGER NOT NULL,
    window_end INTEGER NOT NULL,
    candles INTEGER NOT NULL,
    status TEXT NOT NULL,           -- ok | not_found
    source TEXT NOT NULL,
    methodology_version TEXT NOT NULL,
    fetched_ts INTEGER NOT NULL,
    PRIMARY KEY (product_id, granularity, window_start)
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS archive_runs (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    started_ts INTEGER NOT NULL,
    finished_ts INTEGER,
    granularity INTEGER NOT NULL,
    range_start INTEGER NOT NULL,
    range_end INTEGER NOT NULL,
    products INTEGER NOT NULL,
    requests INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    candles_written INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    windows_skipped INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    rate_limited INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    errors INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'running',
    note TEXT
);
"""

META = {
    "purpose": (
        "Raw Coinbase Exchange candles for periods the live collector missed. "
        "Not live collection and not a replay of Moonwalking signals."
    ),
    "source": SOURCE,
    "methodology_version": METHODOLOGY_VERSION,
    "absent_minute_means": "no trades in that interval (inside a fetched window)",
}


def default_path() -> str:
    return os.getenv("MW_CANDLE_ARCHIVE_DB") or os.path.join(
        os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "data", "candle_archive.sqlite"
    )


def connect(path: str | None = None) -> sqlite3.Connection:
    path = path or default_path()
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(path)), exist_ok=True)
    conn = sqlite3.connect(path, timeout=30)
    conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
    conn.executescript(SCHEMA)
    conn.executemany(
        "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO archive_meta (key, value) VALUES (?, ?)",
        META.items(),
    )
    conn.commit()
    return conn


def windows(start_ts: int, end_ts: int, granularity: int) -> Iterator[tuple[int, int]]:
    """Request windows of 300 candles covering [start_ts, end_ts].

    Windows sit on a fixed grid (multiples of 300 candles since the epoch), so
    runs over overlapping ranges ask for the same windows and a re-run can
    skip what's already archived. Coinbase treats start and end as
    inclusive, so a window is [s, s + 299 * g].
    """
    if granularity not in GRANULARITIES:
        raise ValueError(f"unsupported granularity {granularity}")
    step = MAX_CANDLES_PER_REQUEST * granularity
    s = start_ts - start_ts % step
    while s <= end_ts:
        yield s, s + step - granularity
        s += step


class RateLimited(Exception):
    pass


@dataclass
class Pacer:
    """Keeps requests at or under ``rate`` per second, and halves the rate for
    a while after a 429 so the live collector (same IP, same limit) is never
    the one that gets throttled."""

    rate: float = 2.0
    min_rate: float = 0.25
    _interval: float = field(init=False)
    _next: float = field(init=False, default=0.0)

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        self._interval = 1.0 / self.rate

    def wait(self) -> None:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now < self._next:
            time.sleep(self._next - now)
        self._next = max(now, self._next) + self._interval

    def backoff(self) -> float:
        self._interval = min(self._interval * 2, 1.0 / self.min_rate)
        self._next = time.monotonic() + self._interval * 4
        return self._interval

    def recover(self) -> None:
        self._interval = max(1.0 / self.rate, self._interval * 0.95)


def _iso(ts: int) -> str:
    return time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime(ts))


def fetch(session, product_id: str, granularity: int, start_ts: int, end_ts: int):
    """One request. Returns candle tuples (ts, open, high, low, close, volume),
    or None if Coinbase doesn't know the product."""
    resp = session.get(
        CANDLES_URL.format(product_id=product_id),
        params={"granularity": granularity, "start": _iso(start_ts), "end": _iso(end_ts)},
        timeout=15,
    )
    if resp.status_code == 429:
        raise RateLimited(product_id)
    if resp.status_code == 404:
        return None
    resp.raise_for_status()
    rows = []
    for entry in resp.json():
        # Coinbase order: [time, low, high, open, close, volume]
        ts, low, high, open_, close, volume = entry
        ts = int(ts)
        if start_ts <= ts <= end_ts:
            rows.append((ts, float(open_), float(high), float(low), float(close), float(volume)))
    return rows


@dataclass
class RunStats:
    requests: int = 0
    candles_written: int = 0
    windows_skipped: int = 0
    rate_limited: int = 0
    errors: int = 0


def archive_product(
    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
    session,
    pacer: Pacer,
    product_id: str,
    granularity: int,
    start_ts: int,
    end_ts: int,
    stats: RunStats,
    max_attempts: int = 6,
    now_ts: int | None = None,
) -> None:
    """Fetch every window for one product that isn't already archived.
    Re-running is cheap: fetched windows are skipped."""
    done = {
        row[0]
        for row in conn.execute(
            "SELECT window_start FROM fetched_windows WHERE product_id = ? AND granularity = ?",
            (product_id, granularity),
        )
    }
    for w_start, w_end in windows(start_ts, end_ts, granularity):
        if w_start in done:
            stats.windows_skipped += 1
            continue
        for attempt in range(max_attempts):
            pacer.wait()
            stats.requests += 1
            try:
                rows = fetch(session, product_id, granularity, w_start, w_end)
                pacer.recover()
                break
            except RateLimited:
                stats.rate_limited += 1
                interval = pacer.backoff()
                logger.warning("429 on %s; slowing to one request per %.1fs", product_id, interval)
            except (requests.RequestException, ValueError) as exc:
                stats.errors += 1
                logger.warning("fetch failed %s %s: %s", product_id, _iso(w_start), exc)
                time.sleep(min(30, 2 ** attempt))
        else:
            # Leave the window unrecorded so the next run retries it.
            logger.error("giving up on %s window %s for this run", product_id, _iso(w_start))
            continue
        now = int(time.time()) if now_ts is None else now_ts
        status = "not_found" if rows is None else "ok"
        rows = rows or []
        # A window still open (its last candle isn't closed yet) is stored but
        # not marked fetched, so the next run completes it.
        closed = w_end + granularity <= now
        with conn:
            conn.executemany(
                "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO candles "
                "(product_id, granularity, ts, open, high, low, close, volume) "
                "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                [(product_id, granularity, *r) for r in rows],
            )
            if closed or status == "not_found":
                conn.execute(
                    "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO fetched_windows "
                    "(product_id, granularity, window_start, window_end, candles, status, "
                    "source, methodology_version, fetched_ts) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                    (product_id, granularity, w_start, w_end, len(rows), status,
                     SOURCE, METHODOLOGY_VERSION, now),
                )
        stats.candles_written += len(rows)
        if status == "not_found":
            return  # every later window would 404 too


def run(
    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
    products: Iterable[str],
    granularity: int,
    start_ts: int,
    end_ts: int,
    *,
    session=None,
    pacer: Pacer | None = None,
    note: str | None = None,
    progress=None,
    now_ts: int | None = None,
) -> RunStats:
    products = list(products)
    session = session or requests.Session()
    pacer = pacer or Pacer()
    stats = RunStats()
    with conn:
        run_id = conn.execute(
            "INSERT INTO archive_runs (started_ts, granularity, range_start, range_end, products, note) "
            "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
            (int(time.time()), granularity, start_ts, end_ts, len(products), note),
        ).lastrowid
    status = "error"
    try:
        for i, product_id in enumerate(products, 1):
            archive_product(
                conn, session, pacer, product_id, granularity, start_ts, end_ts, stats, now_ts=now_ts
            )
            if progress:
                progress(i, len(products), product_id, stats)
            _save_run(conn, run_id, stats, "running")
        status = "ok" if stats.errors == 0 else "ok_with_errors"
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        status = "interrupted"
        raise
    finally:
        _save_run(conn, run_id, stats, status, finished=True)
    return stats


def _save_run(conn, run_id, stats: RunStats, status: str, finished: bool = False) -> None:
    with conn:
        conn.execute(
            "UPDATE archive_runs SET requests = ?, candles_written = ?, windows_skipped = ?, "
            "rate_limited = ?, errors = ?, status = ?, finished_ts = ? WHERE id = ?",
            (stats.requests, stats.candles_written, stats.windows_skipped, stats.rate_limited,
             stats.errors, status, int(time.time()) if finished else None, run_id),
        )


def usd_products(session=None) -> list[str]:
    """Every USD-quoted product Coinbase Exchange lists right now."""
    session = session or requests.Session()
    resp = session.get(PRODUCTS_URL, timeout=15)
    resp.raise_for_status()
    return sorted(
        p["id"] for p in resp.json() if str(p.get("quote_currency")).upper() == "USD"
    )


def collection_gaps(
    outcomes_db: str, min_gap_seconds: int = 3600
) -> list[tuple[int, int]]:
    """Periods with no live board activity, from the board outcome store.
    Board entries arrive every few minutes while the collector runs, so a
    silence longer than ``min_gap_seconds`` means it wasn't running."""
    conn = sqlite3.connect(f"file:{outcomes_db}?mode=ro", uri=True)
    try:
        ts = [r[0] for r in conn.execute(
            "SELECT DISTINCT entered_ts FROM board_outcomes_v2 ORDER BY entered_ts")]
    finally:
        conn.close()
    return [(a, b) for a, b in zip(ts, ts[1:]) if b - a > min_gap_seconds]
